THE LIMIT: Findmypast holds only eight Queensland commonwealth years and the last is 1959, so for a family that arrived in 1949 there is ONE usable roll. The near-annual run this archive was promised is at the NLA and on Ancestry, not here.
16 Sep 2026NAA — the Ugolino Vivaldi manifest of 1 March 1951, read in full doneAll 25 images of NAA K269, item 30133197: the Lloyd Triestino master list of incoming passengers for trip 2/51, and the Department of Immigration's schedule of alien passengers for debarkation at Fremantle. Searched for MAZZA, for PROSTAMO, and for any accompanying child.IT DISPROVES THE IDENTIFICATION THIS ARCHIVE HAS CARRIED SINCE THE CROSSINGS WERE FIRST WORKED. ONE MAZZA aboard: «MAZZA Domenico», no. 476, Alien, bound for NARRACAN, VICTORIA. NO PROSTAMO aboard under any spelling -- the P sequence runs PRATICO', PREBIANCA, PRELL, POTH, PUGLISI with no gap. NO SECOND MAZZA, and the list names children individually, so a two-year-old Salvatore would have been on it. THE PORT COLUMN IS THE PROOF: unlabelled in the master list, but every -F- passenger appears in the separate Fremantle debarkation schedule and every -M- has a Victorian address, so F is Fremantle and M is Melbourne. MAZZA Domenico is -M- and he is ABSENT from the Fremantle schedule, which runs MARTINELLI straight to MERENDINO. DOMENICA PROSTAMO WAS GOING TO HER HUSBAND IN BRISBANE; this man landed at Melbourne for a Gippsland shire. The candidate is withdrawn and her March 1951 crossing is unknown again. THE INDEX COULD NEVER HAVE SHOWN THIS. It gave a name, a date and a ship; the manifest gave a destination and a port of debarkation, and those are what broke it.

THE CAUTION: an NAA item title is a FINDING AID, not a record that has been read. It is transcribed from the file and it is reliable, but nothing here has been opened -- all of it is paper, most of it at Brisbane, and J25 1971/1074 is «not yet examined» and needs an access request. 16 Sep 2026NAA — Mario Crupi's aliens registration card, read in full doneNAA: D4028, CRUPI MARIO, item 9209611 — digitised, two sides, read. Found by sweeping the NAA name index for CRUPI, POLISTENA and ARENA.THE MOST DETAILED DOCUMENT THIS ARCHIVE HAS ON ANY AUSTRALIAN MEMBER OF THIS FAMILY, and it is free and digitised. N.B.E. certificate Q30124. MARIO CRUPI, born 28 NOVEMBER 1905 at PIEDIMONTE, PROVINCE OF CATANIA. Cane cutter. Address Bemerside P.O., INGHAM. Entered Australia 13 JUNE 1928, PORT OF DEBARKATION BRISBANE. Five feet eight, brown eyes, black hair, heavy build; SCAR ON LEFT THIGH, WART ON RIGHT SIDE OF FACE. THE REVERSE IS THE PART THAT MATTERS: «18.8.42 LOVEDAY INTERNMENT CAMP. 30/12/43 Released and entrained for Brisbane.» HE WAS INTERNED FOR SIXTEEN MONTHS. THAT IS TWO MEN IN THIS FAMILY INTERNED IN 1942 -- Rosario Mazza on his own dossier, and now his wife's brother. THE STRUCTURAL FINDING IS THE BIRTHPLACE. PIEDIMONTE IS THE MAZZA'S OWN TOWN. Rosario Mazza was born at Piedimonte Etneo in 1899 and married Angela Crupi, born about 1900 in the province of Catania -- and here is her brother, born at Piedimonte Etneo in 1905. THE CRUPI AND THE MAZZA CAME FROM THE SAME VILLAGE, which this archive did not know and which is how these marriages were made. THE IDENTIFICATION IS STRONG AND IS NOT PROVED: the card names no parents. It rests on the surname, the birthplace, the north Queensland cane districts where the Crupi brothers appear on the electoral rolls, and the 1989 Queensland death of a Mario Crupi whose parents are Francesco Crupi and Maria Vecchio.
20 Sep 2026Antenati name index — every Arena death at Scilla, 1910-1943 doneAntenati's «cerca per nome» index, cognome=Arena, localita=Scilla: 69 indexed persons, of whom 26 are the deceased and the rest appear as the parents and spouses named in those same acts.THE INSTRUMENT THIS ARCHIVE HAS BEEN WORKING WITHOUT. Antenati NAME-INDEXES Scilla's death acts from about 1910 to 1943, with FATHER, MOTHER AND SPOUSE on each one, and it can be queried directly -- so a death that would have cost a volume list, a container lookup, a manifest fetch and an index read is now one search. THE THREE THAT MATTER. ROCCO ARENA, the New York emigrant, DIED AT SCILLA ON 8 FEBRUARY 1932: «Padre Francesco Antonio, Madre Serafina Arlotta, Coniuge Generosa Costa», all three matching this archive exactly. GIOVANNI ARENA, his brother, DIED AT SCILLA ON 31 MARCH 1936, «Madre Serafina Arlotta, Coniuge Concetta Russo» -- he sailed to join Rocco in 1899 and he came home, which is why he baptised no child in New York. SERAFINA ARENA, born in Manhattan on 15 August 1898, DIED AT SCILLA ON 15 AUGUST 1912. AND A WHOLE HOUSEHOLD FALLS OUT AS A BY-PRODUCT: Orazio Arena and Giovanna Zirilli's children TERESA (d. 1913, wife of Pietro Bova), GIUSEPPE (d. 1922, husband of Fortunata Maria Pontillo), MARIA ANNUNZIATA (d. 1933, wife of Rocco Chirico) and ALESSANDRO ANTONIO (d. 1936, husband of Maria Antonia Lico) -- plus a DOMENICO ARENA (d. 1923) whose mother is MARIANTONIA MUSOLINO, which means Orazio married twice. NOTE WHAT IS ABSENT AND WHAT IT PROVES: FRANCESCO ANTONIO ARENA and SERAFINA ARLOTTA are named repeatedly as parents but never as the deceased, so they did not die inside 1910-1943. The archive had already read the annual index for 1889-1896 and not found them. THEY DIED BETWEEN 1897 AND 1909 -- row 69 goes from forty-seven unread years to THIRTEEN. ALL TWENTY-SIX WRITTEN OUT, 20 SEPTEMBER, AND THE SUMMARY HAD BEEN HIDING THINGS. This row named eight of the twenty-six deceased and moved on. The answer to a question asked four days later -- whose son was the Filippo Arena of the 1904 act -- was in the other eighteen all along: FILIPPO ARENA, d. 17 March 1915, «Padre Giovanni Arena, Madre Natalina Longordo, Coniuge Clementina Furfari». The full twenty-six are now in scilla-reads, and they resolve the surname into AT LEAST SEVEN SCILLA HOUSEHOLDS. A SELECTIVE RECORD OF A COMPLETE SEARCH COSTS THE SEARCH TWICE. Where a query returns a bounded set, write the set down.
16 Sep 2026Antenati name index — Donato, Arlotta and Polistena deaths at Scilla doneAntenati's name index, localita=Scilla, for DONATO (11 deceased), ARLOTTA (24) and POLISTENA (25) — each act carrying father, mother and spouse.FOUR WORK-LIST ROWS ANSWERED FROM ONE INSTRUMENT. ROW 70, CLOSED: MICHELE DONATO died at Scilla on 10 MAY 1936, «Padre Domenico, Madre Nunziata Sofi, Coniuge Maria Sofi». He was the last man on the Donato line whose end was unknown; he was fifty-two on the 1908 New Orleans manifest and lived another twenty-eight years after coming home. ROW 96 AND ROW 41, BOTH CLOSED BY ONE ACT: CAMILLO ANTONIO POLISTENA died 19 OCTOBER 1924, «Padre GIUSEPPE, Madre ANNA PONTILLO, Coniuge GIOVANNA ROMANO». That gives the thinnest quarter TWO NEW ANCESTORS -- and it explains the archive's oldest unresolved conflict. The tree calls Giovanni Polistena's mother «Giovanna Pontillo»; three records already called her ROMANO; here is a fourth, and here is where the Pontillo came from -- IT IS HIS GRANDMOTHER'S SURNAME, one generation up, carried down onto the wrong woman. The tree's «Giuseppi Pontillo» is Camillo Antonio's own father GIUSEPPE POLISTENA. ROW 4, PREDICTION CONFIRMED: the row reasoned from naming custom that Domenico Donato and Nunziata Sofi should have a PASQUALE, for the maternal grandfather Pasquale Sofi. PASQUALE DONATO died 20 APRIL 1937 with both parents named. ROW 39, THREE SIBLINGS FOR SERAFINA ARLOTTA: DOMENICO (d. 14 March 1915), ANNA (d. 16 March 1915) and GIUSEPPE ANTONIO (d. 2 June 1918), all «Padre Giovanni Arlotta, Madre Maria Costa». Domenico and Anna died TWO DAYS APART, which is worth reading the acts for. AND ONE SMALL CORRECTION: Teressa Polistena died 5 JANUARY 1933, not 1934.
16 Sep 2026Antenati name index — what Scilla actually has indexed, and for which years doneThe whole Scilla name index with no surname filter: 13,777 indexed persons. Read the year facet across its full span, then pulled every act for 1880, 1902 and 1909 to see what type they are.IT IS DEATHS, AND ONLY DEATHS, AND THE ANSWER DEFLATES FOUR ROWS WHILE SHARPENING THEM. Every act in the index, across every year from 1880 to 1943, is an ATTO DI MORTE. There are NO BIRTHS and NO MARRIAGES in it. THE COVERAGE IS 1911 TO 1943, SOLIDLY -- between 279 and 704 indexed persons a year -- with only a SCATTER BEFORE THAT: four persons in 1880, one in 1885, and a handful each in 1902, 1905, 1906, 1908, 1909 and 1910. WHAT THAT COSTS. The marriage of Nunziata Donato and Giovanni Polistena about 1916 is NOT reachable by query and stays a volume read; so is Camillo Antonio Polistena's birth about 1835, and so are the Donato births of 1845-1860. THE HOPE THAT ONE QUERY WOULD ANSWER THEM IS GONE. WHAT IT GAINS. The window it does cover, 1911-1943, is exactly when the Italian relatives of this family's emigrants were dying, which is why it gave up Rocco Arena, his brother Giovanni, Michele Donato and Camillo Antonio Polistena in an afternoon. AND TWO CAUTIONS. «anno=» is the year parameter and it is control-tested -- anno=1880 returns 4, matching the facet exactly, while «estremoRemoto=» is silently ignored and returns all 13,777. AND THE PRE-1911 STRAGGLERS ARE NOT A BACK DOOR INTO THE MISSING VOLUMES: the two acts indexed under 1909 carry document arks an_ud16595370 and an_ud16595649, which fall INSIDE the ark ranges of the 1911 and 1912 volumes. They are 1909 deaths written into later registers, not a 1909 volume that browse cannot see. A NOTE ON SIZE: 13,777 indexed persons is not 13,777 deaths. Each act indexes the deceased plus two or three relatives, so the real figure is nearer a quarter of that.
16 Sep 2026Antenati name index — every Costa death at Scilla, 1880-1943 doneAntenati's name index, cognome=Costa, localita=Scilla: 76 indexed persons, 18 of them the deceased.IT CLOSED THE COUSIN QUESTION AND OPENED TWO GENERATIONS. GENEROSA COSTA, Rocco Arena's wife, DIED AT SCILLA ON 6 JUNE 1916 -- «Padre ANTONINO COSTA, Madre ANNA ARLOTTA, Coniuge ROCCO ARENA». She died sixteen years before her husband. HER FATHER'S OWN ACT IS HERE TOO: ANTONIO COSTA, died 1 JUNE 1915, «Padre LORENZO COSTA, Madre GENEROSA POLISTENA» -- TWO NEW ANCESTORS on a line that stopped dead at him, and the reason for his daughter's name, since the first daughter takes the paternal grandmother's. IT ALSO PUTS A POLISTENA INSIDE THE COSTA LINE a generation early. AND THE COUSIN CLAIM IS NOW CERTAIN. Four acts agree: Anna Arlotta's death names her parents as Giovanni Arlotta and Maria Costa, who are Serafina Arlotta's parents; her husband's death names her back; Generosa's own death names that couple as her parents; and the 1888 marriage gave them first. ANNA AND SERAFINA WERE SISTERS, SO ROCCO AND GENEROSA WERE FIRST COUSINS. ANNA DIED ON 16 MARCH 1915 AND ANTONINO ON 1 JUNE, eleven weeks apart, in the same year her brother Domenico died two days after her.
16 Sep 2026Antenati name index — Sofi and Zirilli at Scilla donecognome=Sofi (64 indexed persons, 20 of them the deceased) and cognome=Zirilli (10 indexed persons) at Scilla.TWO NEGATIVES WORTH HAVING AND ONE STRUCTURAL FACT. NO ZIRILLI IS EVER THE DECEASED. All ten Zirilli in the index are mothers named inside somebody else's act, and GIOVANNA ZIRILLI -- Orazio Arena's wife -- appears FIVE TIMES that way: mother of Teresa Arena (d. 1913), Giuseppe Arena (d. 1922), Maria Annunziata Arena (d. 1933), Alessandro Antonio Arena (d. 1936) and a Mariantonia Costa (d. 1919). SHE DIED BEFORE 1911, which is the same reading as Francesco Antonio Arena and Serafina Arlotta. MARIA SOFI'S PARENTS ARE NOT HERE. Row 72 wants them; the index holds four women called Maria Sofi and NOT ONE is named as the wife of a Michele Donato. The one who died on 3 August 1913 carries no relations at all. The row stays open and stays a volume read. AND THE INDEX DOES NOT DEDUPLICATE PEOPLE -- this is the fact to carry forward. Each act generates its own person-record, so «Giovanna Zirilli» is five records and not one woman linked to five acts, and «69 Arena at Scilla» is sixty-nine MENTIONS across acts, not sixty-nine people. Anyone counting heads off this index will count wrong. NOTED IN PASSING: two Domenico Sofi, both «Padre Giorgio, Madre Maria Paladino», dying in 1933 and 1934 -- the same repeated-name pattern this archive reads as a child who died, except both of these reached adulthood, so it wants an act rather than an assumption. MARIA PALADINO is also the name in dispute at row 25, and this is a different family.
16 Sep 2026Antenati name index — Romano, Russo, Martello, Paladino and Pontillo at Scilla partcognome=Romano (26 deceased), Russo (12), Martello, Paladino and Pontillo at Scilla. Martello and Paladino both hit the 100-row page cap and were not fully read.THE ROMANO SWEEP FINISHED A LONG ARGUMENT. GIOVANNA ROMANO DIED AT SCILLA ON 15 JULY 1930 -- «Padre ANNUNZIATO, Madre ANGELA ZIRILLI, Coniuge CAMILLO ANTONIO POLISTENA». Her own act, her own surname, her own parents, her own husband. That is the FIFTH record calling her Romano and the first to give her parents, and it retires the tree's «Giuseppi Pontillo» and «Nunziatta Pontillo» for good. TWO MORE NEW ANCESTORS: ANNUNZIATO ROMANO and ANGELA ZIRILLI. AND A THREAD WORTH PULLING: Orazio Arena's wife was GIOVANNA ZIRILLI and Giovanna Romano's mother was ANGELA ZIRILLI -- two Zirilli women marrying into two of the four quarters, which in a town this size is more likely kinship than coincidence and is not yet evidence of it. NEGATIVE ON THE RUSSO: the only Concetta Russo in the index died on 20 May 1939 as the wife of a DOMENICO ROMANO, so she is not Giovanni Arena's wife. His own 1936 act names Concetta Russo as his spouse, so she either outlived the indexed span or died before 1911. PART, NOT DONE: Martello and Paladino returned the full hundred rows the page allows and will need paging or a given-name filter before either can be called searched.
16 Sep 2026Antenati name index — which of this family's comuni are indexed at all doneQueried the name index by locality alone for every comune this family touches: Scilla, Pizzo, Piedimonte Etneo, Briatico, Zambrone, San Costantino Calabro, Potenzoni and Catania — reading the act-type and year facets on each.THE INDEX IS PATCHY BY COMUNE AND BY ACT TYPE, AND ASSUMING OTHERWISE WOULD HAVE COST A WEEK. INDEXED: SCILLA, deaths only, solid 1911-1943 with a scatter back to 1880. PIZZO, BIRTHS only, about 5,900 of them across 1868-1903 -- so «deaths only» was a SCILLA fact and not an Antenati one, and anyone generalising from one comune will guess wrong about the next. CATANIA, deaths, 34,294 from 1911. NOT INDEXED AT ALL, all returning «Nessun risultato trovato»: PIEDIMONTE ETNEO, BRIATICO, ZAMBRONE, SAN COSTANTINO CALABRO and POTENZONI. THAT IS THE WHOLE CALABRIAN SIDE EXCEPT PIZZO AND SCILLA, and the whole Sicilian side except Catania city. The Prostamo, the Melluso, the Anile and the Ventrice cannot be reached this way and stay volume reads. THE PORTAL SAYS SO ITSELF on a nil result: «solo una parte dei registri disponibili e stata completamente indicizzata». TWO NEGATIVES INSIDE THE INDEXED COMUNI: no PROSTAMO among Pizzo's indexed births, which is expected since the family was at Briatico by the 1868-1903 window; and no MAZZA or NICOTRA of this family at Catania. AND A CAUTION ABOUT THE PARAMETER ITSELF: «localita=» does NOT mean «the act was registered here». It matches ANY place attached to the person, birth or death, and apparently the historical province too -- localita=Catania returns people who died at VICOPISANO IN TUSCANY and at Catenanuova. It worked cleanly at Scilla only because the people there were born and died in the same town.
16 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, the pre-1866 death series listed doneThe whole Stato civile della restaurazione death series for Scilla, listed in one page load via tipologia=Morti on serie 2002440 with s_size=100.FIFTY-ONE VOLUMES, 1817 TO 1865, AND TWO ROWS THAT COULD NOT START NOW CAN. The archive held 55 Nati and 54 Matrimoni volumes for this period and NOT ONE Morti volume; the series had simply never been listed. NOTE THE START YEAR -- AND NOTE THAT IT WAS WRONG. This row said the births begin in 1816; SCILLA'S REGISTERS BEGIN IN 1809, in a napoleonic fondo this archive had not then listed. Within the restaurazione fondo alone the births begin in 1816 and the DEATHS BEGIN IN 1817, so either Scilla registered no deaths in its first year or that volume is gone. Anyone reasoning «civil registration begins in 1816» is reasoning SEVEN years wrong for a birth and EIGHT for a death. DOUBLED YEARS: 1846 and 1854 each have two volumes. WHAT IT UNBLOCKS: FILIPPO ARENA and DOMENICA COSTA, both born in the 1790s and both alive when their son married in 1851, so dying most likely in the 1850s to 1870s -- the back half of this run and the front of the post-1866 one, and the only remaining route to a generation above Filippo. And DOMENICO ARLOTTA, the deepest name on the Scilla side, dead before April 1854 and therefore inside this run if he died after 1817. THE SERIES ID IS THE REUSABLE PART: serie 2002440 carries ALL THREE act types for the restaurazione period and is filtered by tipologia, exactly as 16594786 does for the italiano period.
16 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Morti 1924, parte I, atto 83, read in full doneThe death act of Camillo Antonio Polistena, found from the volume's own INDICE ANNUALE and read at 1800px. Container 5xGkdq3, image 45 of 75.THE FIRST OF THE FOUR ACTS THE INDEX ONLY SUMMARISED, AND IT CORRECTS THE ARCHIVE BY NINE YEARS. «L'anno millenovecento ventiquattro addi diciannove di Ottobre, a ore otto e minuti quaranta ... e morto POLISTENA CAMILLO ANTONIO, di anni OTTANTA, PESCATORE, residente in questo Comune, nato in Scilla, dal fu GIUSEPPE, e dalla fu PONTILLO ANNA, MARITO DI ROMANO GIOVANNA.» AGED EIGHTY IN OCTOBER 1924 MEANS BORN ABOUT 1844. The archive carried «about 1835», which was inferred from his son's birth rather than read. HE DIED AT FIFTY MINUTES PAST MIDNIGHT at the house in RIONE MONACINA and the act was registered the same morning. THE DECLARANTS ARE BOTH FISHERMEN: Matra Camillo fu Giacomo, sixty-three, and POLISTENA ANTONIO FU LUIGI, twenty-eight -- a Polistena kinsman this archive does not hold. NOT THE ROCCO ARENA OF THIS FAMILY, who was sixty-three in 1924 and the son of Francesco Antonio -- this is the Rocco Arena son of Filippo and Clementina Furfari who died in 1938, and he is exactly the namesake row 132 warns about. THE METHOD IS THE REUSABLE PART: the volume's INDICE ANNUALE at image 74 gives «Polistena Camillo Antonio fu Giuseppe, parte 1a, numero 83», and the acts run TWO PER IMAGE, so act 83 is image 45. That is three downloads instead of seventy-five.
16 Sep 2026NAA — Mario Crupi's internment file and naturalisation record, read doneNameSearch for CRUPI Mario across all record categories: twelve items. The internee dossier NAA: MP1103/1, Q30124 is digitised and was read; the naturalisation record NAA: A714, 9/4300 carries its detail in the item title itself.HE WAS NATURALISED IN APRIL 1940 AND INTERNED IN APRIL 1942 ANYWAY, AND THAT IS THE FINDING. AND IT PROVES SOMETHING THIS ARCHIVE REFUSED TO CLAIM. The 1959 electoral roll put a Mario and a Maria Crupi at Tully with the address column blank on both, and the refusal to pair them on adjacency alone was right; the next-of-kin line pairs them properly. IT ALSO DATES THE MARRIAGE to between 26 April 1940, when he was SINGLE, and 20 April 1942, when he was married. FIVE MORE FILES ARE NOT YET OPENED: BP242/1 Q25743, his Queensland investigation case file; A435 1946/4/2800; D1901 C594; MP1103/2 Q30124; and J25 1955/16646.
16 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Morti 1932, parte I, atto 9, read in full doneRocco Arena's death act, found from the volume's INDICE ANNUALE («Arena Rocco fu Francesco Antonio, parte Ia, numero 9») and read at 1800px. Container w6zPMA1, image 8 of 63.THE SECOND OF THE FOUR ACTS, AND IT CLOSES THE AMERICAN STORY IN THE PLACE IT STARTED. «e morto ARENA ROCCO, di anni SETTANTUNO, PESCATORE, residente in Scilla, nato in questo Comune, dal fu FRANCESCO ANTONIO e dalla fu ARLOTTA SERAFINA, VEDOVO DI COSTA GENEROSA.» HE DIED AT A QUARTER PAST SIX IN THE EVENING ON 8 FEBRUARY 1932, at the house in VIA PROF. ZAGARI, and the act was drawn the following morning. A FISHERMAN AND A WIDOWER. Generosa Costa had died in June 1916 and he outlived her by fifteen years and eight months. EVERY NAME AGREES WITH THE ARCHIVE: both parents, the wife, the town. WHAT IS NEW IS THE ADDRESS, THE TRADE AND THE HOUR. ONE SMALL TENSION, RECORDED RATHER THAN SMOOTHED: «di anni settantuno» would put his birth before February 1861, but his birth act is Nati 1861 ATTO 228, late in the year, and the 1900 US census says September 1861 -- which makes him SEVENTY. The birth act wins; an age on a death act is what two elderly neighbours told the registrar. THE COMPANY HE DIED AMONG IS WORTH NOTING: the declarants and witnesses are PONTILLO ROCCO FU GIOVANNI, LAMERI GIUSEPPE FU LUIGI, PONTILLO SANTO FU GIOVANNI and ALFONZETTI ROCCO FU ANTONINO -- two Pontillo brothers among them, in a street of fishermen, all four signing with a mark.
16 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Morti 1936, parte I, atti 14 and 20, read in full doneThe death acts of Giovanni Arena and Michele Donato, both in the 1936 volume, found from its INDICE ANNUALE and read. Container 0AVjBab, images 10 and 13 of 57.THE LAST TWO OF THE FOUR ACTS, AND THEY PUT TWO QUARTERS OF THIS FAMILY ON THE SAME STREET. GIOVANNI ARENA, atto 14: died 31 MARCH 1936 at nine in the morning, VIA GROTTE 72, «di anni OTTANTUNO, MARINAIO, dal fu ANTONIO e dalla fu ARLOTTA SERAFINA, MARITO DI RUSSO CONCETTA». The age is exact -- born 30 April 1854, he was eighty-one and a month short of eighty-two. His wife was still living. MICHELE DONATO, atto 20: died 10 MAY 1936 at six in the morning, VIA GROTTE 66, «di anni OTTANTA, MARINAIO, dal fu DOMENICO e da fu SOFI NUNZIATA, MARITO DI SOFI MARIA». SIXTY YARDS AND SIX WEEKS APART. AND ONE CORRECTION: the tree has Michele Donato born in 1851, which would make him eighty-five here and fifty-seven on the New Orleans manifest of 1908 that calls him fifty-two. THE DEATH ACT AND THE MANIFEST BOTH POINT AT 1855 OR 1856 and the tree is five years out. THE STREET IS THE LARGER FINDING AND IT WAS ALREADY IN THE ARCHIVE UNNOTICED: Fortunato Arena born at VIA GROTTE 70 in 1891, Giuseppe Arena at 75 in 1892, Maria Donato at 21 in 1888, Giovanni Polistena at 47 in 1883. THREE OF THE FOUR SCILLA QUARTERS LIVED ON ONE STREET, which is how they kept marrying each other. BOTH ACTS ALSO SHARE THEIR WITNESSES -- Mulinaro Domenico, twenty-two, a driver, and Delorenzo Rocco, thirty-three, an attendant -- who are municipal regulars rather than family.
17 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Morti 1912, parte I, atto 83, read in full doneThe act behind the index line this archive read as Serafina Arena's death. Found from the volume's own INDICE — «Arena Serafina, di Rocco, Agosto 15, Numero 83, Parte 1a, Pagina 22» — and read. Container LeplDll, image 45 of 93.IT IS NOT THE SERAFINA THIS ARCHIVE THOUGHT, AND THE ROW BUILT ON IT IS RETRACTED. «e morta ARENA SERAFINA, DI MESI TRE, residente in Scilla, NATA IN QUESTO COMUNE, da ROCCO, marinaro, domiciliato in Scilla, e da GENEROSA COSTA, casalinga.» THREE MONTHS OLD AND BORN AT SCILLA. Yesterday this archive recorded that Serafina Arena, baptised at Our Lady of Loretto in Manhattan on 15 August 1898, had died at Scilla on her FOURTEENTH BIRTHDAY. The matching dates were what made it persuasive and they were a coincidence. WHAT MISLED ME IS WORTH NAMING: Antenati's name index prints «Nascita: Scilla» on these entries and I decided that field was a default rather than a claim, because believing it was inconvenient. IT WAS A CLAIM AND IT WAS CORRECT. WHAT IS FOUND INSTEAD IS A CHILD NOBODY HERE KNEW OF: Rocco Arena and Generosa Costa had a SECOND SERAFINA, born at Scilla about May 1912 in the QUARTIERE CHIANALEA, dead at three months. THEY GAVE THE NAME AGAIN FOURTEEN YEARS AFTER THE FIRST, which is the same reading this archive makes of their two sons named Antonio. AND IT PROVES SOMETHING ABOUT THE HOUSEHOLD: they were at Scilla and still having children in 1912, which is the return from America seen from the inside rather than inferred from three absences. GENEROSA COSTA WAS ABOUT FORTY-FOUR. THE NEW YORK SERAFINA HAS NO KNOWN FATE AGAIN. The act also gives Rocco as MARINARO, where his own death act of 1932 calls him PESCATORE.
17 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Morti 1915, parte I, atti 34 and 36, read in full doneThe death acts of Domenico and Anna Arlotta, two days apart in March 1915, found from the volume's own INDICE and read. Container Lq2qDbM, images 20 and 21 of 95.THE ACTS CONFIRM THE PARENTS AND DEFLATE MY OWN FRAMING OF THE DATES. DOMENICO ARLOTTA, atto 34: died at noon on 14 MARCH 1915 at the RIONE CHIANALEA, «di anni SETTANTATRE, MARINARO ... da fu GIOVANNI e da fu COSTA MARIA, marito di PONTILLO MARIA». ANNA ARLOTTA, atto 36: died at four in the morning on 16 MARCH 1915 at the QUARTIERE CHIANALEA, «di anni OTTANTA, casalinga ... da fu GIOVANNI e da fu COSTA MARIA, MOGLIE DI COSTA ANTONINO». BOTH PARENTS NAMED ON BOTH ACTS, so the sibling link and the cousin chain behind it now rest on the ACTS and not on an index line. Their ages date them: Anna about 1835, Domenico about 1842, against Serafina Arlotta's documented 13 June 1830. THE ROW ASKED WHETHER AN EPIDEMIC OR THE SEA TOOK THEM. NEITHER ACT SAYS, BECAUSE ITALIAN CIVIL DEATH ACTS DO NOT RECORD A CAUSE. AND THE ARITHMETIC TAKES THE DRAMA OUT OF IT: Scilla registered about a hundred and twenty-five deaths in 1915, which is one every three days, so TWO DEATHS IN A WEEK IS THE ORDINARY RATE and a third, ARENA FILIPPO on 17 March, makes three in four days without meaning anything either. WHAT IS ACTUALLY NOTABLE IS NOT THE TIMING BUT THE ADDRESS: both died in CHIANALEA, the fishing quarter -- where ROCCO ARENA AND GENEROSA COSTA'S INFANT DAUGHTER had died three years earlier. An eighty-year-old and her brother of seventy-three, in the same quarter, two days apart, is most simply one household and one illness; the register will not say so and this archive should not either.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, the annual death indexes of 1897–1908 doneThe A section of every annual death index from 1897 to 1908 — twelve years, less 1909, which Antenati does not hold — read at full resolution for FRANCESCO ANTONIO ARENA and SERAFINA ARLOTTA. Containers for all twelve are saved in scilla-arks-italiano.tsv.BOTH FOUND, AND THE SWEEP IS FINISHED. FRANCESCO ANTONIO ARENA died 29 AUGUST 1901 — Morti 1901, parte I, atto 96 — and SERAFINA ARLOTTA died 24 APRIL 1908 — Morti 1908, parte I, atto 55 — BOTH AT VIA GROTTE NUMBER TWO, the same house, six years and eight months apart. Read in full in scilla-reads. THE YEARS THAT WERE CLEAR, kept so no one reads them again. 1897: Arena Nicolina di Filippo (10), two Anastasi, Arbitrio Antonio (111), Arlotta Antonio di Rocco (144), Arlotta Giuseppa di Filippo (185), Andaloro Pasquale (200). 1898: Alfonzetti Concetta (4), Arbitrio Rocco (44), Arena Rocco di Rocco (55), Anastasi Antonio (88), an ARENA FRANCESCO GIOVANNI with no act number written, Arena Rocco di Giuseppe (91), Alfonzetti Maria (102), Alfonzetti Antonia (109). 1899: five entries and no Arena at all. 1900: clear. 1902: ONE ENTRY IN THE WHOLE YEAR — Andaloro Maria Antonia fu Francesco (76) — and not a single Arena or Arlotta death in a town where both are among the commonest names. 1903: Alfonzetti Michelangelo fu Onofrio, Arlotta Rocco fu Orazio, Arlotta Maria di Filippo. 1905: Arlotta Maria fu Antonio (1), Arlotta Rosaria fu Pasquale (12), Arbitrio Giuseppe di Natale (28), Arena Rocco fu Orazio (70), Alfonzetti Giuseppe (113), Andaloro Anna Maria (138). 1907: TWO ENTRIES — Arlotta Antonio fu Orazio (20) and Arbitrio Marianna di Rocco (124). SIX LEADS LEFT LYING, all Arena, none yet read: Arena Raffaele fu Giuseppe (1901, atto 63); ARENA COSMO DI FILIPPO (1904, atto 122) — a LIVING father Filippo, and Rocco Arena's eldest brother Filippo, born 1852, would be fifty-two that year; ARENA NICOLINA DI GIUSEPPE (1906, atto 41) — Rocco's brother Giuseppe, born 1860, married Maria Martello in 1885; Arena Rocco di Alessandro (1906, atto 141); ARENA GIOVANNA DI ROCCO (1908, parte II, atto 2); and Arena Santo fu Rocco (1908, atto 108). THE METHOD, WHICH IS THE PART THAT TRANSFERS. Container ids cost one rate-limited page load each and are SAVED; manifests and images fetch to curl unlimited. The index sits at a different offset in every volume — n−6 to n−9 across these twelve — so tools/antenati_index.py montages the last six to ten images into one contact sheet, read once to locate the A page. THE SCAN OFTEN CROPS THE LEFT PAGE'S SURNAME COLUMN, and the frame BEFORE it re-shoots the same opening whole: that alone rescued 1904 and 1908. Acts run FOUR to an opening, so act-to-image is arithmetic off any one calibration. AND SEE THE CORRECTION FILED WITH THIS ROW: the 1908 index entered Serafina Arlotta under her HUSBAND'S surname, Arena.
17 Sep 2026The National Archives (UK), Discovery — every surname and town of this family doneDiscovery's public JSON API, https://discovery.nationalarchives.gov.uk/API/search/records?sps.searchQuery=..., queried for PROSTAMO, POLISTENA, CRUPI, ARLOTTA, VENTRICE, MELLUSO, CICHELLO, ZIRILLI, FRAGALA, and for the towns SCILLA, BRIATICO and PIEDIMONTE ETNEO.NOTHING HERE FOR THIS FAMILY, AND THE REASON IS STRUCTURAL RATHER THAN BAD LUCK. ZERO RESULTS for PROSTAMO, POLISTENA, CICHELLO, ZIRILLI, FRAGALA, BRIATICO and PIEDIMONTE ETNEO. The few hits on the other names are demonstrably other people: CRUPI returns a Royal Warwickshire medal card for a «Crupie, C A» and a photograph of Monreale cathedral; ARLOTTA returns a merchant seaman born at UDINE in 1934 and a naturalisation for a Maria Arlotta Cook born 1923; MELLUSO returns an academic paper. WHY IT WAS ALWAYS UNLIKELY: this family moved ITALY TO AUSTRALIA and ITALY TO NEW YORK and never through Britain. The Kew series that would matter -- BT 26 and BT 27, the inward and outward passenger lists -- cover UK PORTS ONLY, and every crossing this archive holds left Genoa, Messina or Naples. ONE ANGLE IS NOT CLOSED AND IS WORTH A ROW: TNA holds MERCHANT SEAMEN records that DO carry an Italian birthplace -- BT 372 registry cards and BT 99 crew agreements, the latter showing entries like «place of birth, Napoli» -- and this family is full of MARINAI: Giovanni Arena and Michele Donato are both «marinaio» on their 1936 acts, Domenico Arlotta on his of 1915, Antonino Prostamo «marinaro». If any of them ever signed on a BRITISH-REGISTERED ship they would be in these series. It is a long shot and it is a real one. AND A TRAP WORTH THE RECORD: Discovery throttles with HTTP 202 AND AN EMPTY BODY. A scraper reads that as zero results, which is exactly how a rate limit becomes a published negative -- the same failure shape as Antenati's 403 and Findmypast's dead parameters. Poll until 200 and a non-empty body before believing any count.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Morti 1908 parte II, the transcribed foreign deaths doneParte II of the 1908 Scilla death volume, opened to chase «Arena Giovanna di Rocco» from the annual index — a possible daughter of Rocco Arena.THE LEAD CLOSES AND A WHOLE SERIES OPENS. Arena Giovanna, nine, born at Scilla, died AT MARSEILLE on 1 February 1905; her mother is FRANCESCA MARTELLO, not Generosa Costa, so she is not Rocco Arena's daughter. But PARTE II OF A SCILLA DEATH VOLUME IS A REGISTER OF DEATHS ABROAD, transcribed on the order of the Procuratore del Re at Reggio from certificates issued by foreign authorities, and this archive has never opened one. The 1908 parte II holds five acts and three of them are foreign: NEW YORK (Gulli Rocco, 27, a fractured skull, 17 August 1907, transcribed from an English certificate with a translation at his widow's request), MARSEILLE and TUNIS. Rocco Arena crossed to New York four times and Giovanni Arena followed him in 1899. Parte II sits at the back of each volume immediately before the annual index and is only a few acts long, so eighty-one volumes is an affordable sweep, not a dream.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Morti 1904 atto 122 and 1906 atto 41, the two best Arena leads doneThe two leads out of the 1897–1908 index sweep that named a LIVING father whose name this archive already holds: «Arena Cosmo di Filippo» (1904) and «Arena Nicolina di Giuseppe» (1906). Rocco Arena's brothers are a Filippo born 1852 and a Giuseppe born 1860.ONE CLEAN NEGATIVE AND ONE CANDIDATE THAT NEEDS A MARRIAGE ACT. ARENA NICOLINA IS NOT GIUSEPPE ARENA'S DAUGHTER. She was FORTY when she died on 14 March 1906 at Via Grotte 17 — born about 1866, when Rocco's brother Giuseppe was six. Her father is another Giuseppe Arena, pescatore, still living; her mother the late MARIANNA ROMANO; her husband Giuseppe Cesoriero. That makes a FOURTH distinct Arena household at Scilla. ARENA COSMO IS CLOSED TOO, AN HOUR LATER AND FOR NOTHING. Three months old, dead 1 December 1904 at VICO SAN MICHELE 13, son of FILIPPO, pescatore, and CLEMENTINA FARFARI, lavandaia. THE ACT GIVES THE FATHER NO AGE, so whether this Filippo is Rocco's eldest brother, born 14 February 1852, cannot be settled here. THE TEST IS FILIPPO ARENA'S MARRIAGE ACT, which names his parents; Scilla's civil marriages 1866–1913 are all on Antenati and a man born 1852 married about 1875–1885. A SIDEWAYS PROOF WORTH HAVING: CESORIERO DOMENICO, becchino, is 46 in August 1901 declaring Francesco Antonio Arena's death and 49 in December 1904 declaring this child's — three years and four months, exact. Two gravediggers buried this parish for a decade and their ages hold. RESOLVED BY ONE QUERY INSTEAD OF TEN VOLUMES. The plan was to sweep Scilla's annual MARRIAGE indexes 1874-1885 for Filippo Arena's wedding; seven of those years had no container saved and would have cost a rate-limited page load each before a single image. Instead the ANTENATI NAME INDEX, which carries the SPOUSE, was re-read: FILIPPO ARENA died at Scilla on 17 MARCH 1915, «Padre GIOVANNI ARENA, Madre NATALINA LONGORDO, Coniuge CLEMENTINA FURFARI», and a son of his, ROCCO ARENA, died 31 March 1938 with the same parents. So Cosmo's father is the son of Giovanni Arena and Natalina Longordo, NOT of Francesco Antonio Arena and Serafina Arlotta. ARENA COSMO IS NOT ROCCO ARENA'S NEPHEW, and Rocco's brother Filippo is still missing. THE INDEX ALSO CORRECTED MY TRANSCRIPTION: FURFARI, not Farfari. THE LESSON IS THE ORDER OF OPERATIONS. When a question is «who were this man's parents», ask the NAME INDEX before planning a volume sweep -- it answers with father, mother and spouse in one GET, and it covers 1910-1943, which reaches back over anyone who was alive then.
20 Sep 2026Antenati name index — the six Scilla surnames re-run and WRITTEN OUT IN FULL doneArlotta, Costa, Donato, Polistena, Romano, Pontillo and Musolino at Scilla, «cerca per nome», s_size=100, paced at 4–4.5 seconds and control-tested against «403 Forbidden» on every fetch. All seven had been run before by earlier sessions (rows 128, 130, 131, 132); this pass existed to WRITE THE DECEASED OUT IN FULL rather than summarise them.THE COUNTS. Arlotta 75 records / 24 deceased. Costa 76 / 18. Donato 26 / 11. Polistena 73 / 25. Romano 79 / 26. Pontillo 50 / 24. MUSOLINO 7 / 2. THE NEGATIVE THAT ANSWERS A ROW. MARIANTONIA MUSOLINO — Orazio Arena's apparent second wife — IS NOT IN THE INDEX. Scilla has only seven Musolino records at all and the two deceased are Domenico Musolino (d. 17 April 1911, son of Bruno Musolino and Grazia Longordo) and Francesca Musolino (d. 7 November 1928, father Francesco). Neither is she. She is named only from inside her son's act. So she died outside 1911–1943 or away from Scilla, and the ONLY remaining route to her is Orazio Arena's second marriage act. FOUR HOUSEHOLDS WRITTEN INTO corrections.tsv: Giovanna Romano's three sisters (Maria d. 1917 m. Giovanni Cardona, Concetta d. 1926 m. ROCCO DONATO, Mariantonia d. 1930 m. Matteo Bova); the four children of Lorenzo Costa and Generosa Polistena, including GENEROSA COSTA'S OWN FATHER Antonio, d. 1 June 1915; Domenico Arlotta's wife MARIA PONTILLO, d. 1922, with her parents; and the guard on the two Generosa Costas. FOUR DONATO–POLISTENA TIES, NOT TWO, which is what row 138 wants: Maria Donato d. 1911, wife of GIUSEPPE POLISTINA; Raffaele Polistena x TERESA DONATO, whose children Santa (1926), Maria (1927) and Luigi (1933) all die at Scilla; Giovanni Polistena x ANNUNZIATA DONATO, whose daughter Teresa dies in 1933; and Rocco Polistena, d. 1923, husband of NUNZIATA POLISTENA — a Polistena marrying a Polistena, which is the surname endogamy this archive has already met among the Bueti and the Vizzari. AND THE HONEST COST OF THIS PASS. Every one of these seven surnames had already been queried, days ago, and the results summarised in a sentence apiece. Because they were summarised, they had to be fetched again. THE RULE, NOW STATED TWICE IN ONE DAY: when a query returns a bounded set — twenty-six rows, not twenty-six thousand — WRITE THE SET DOWN. The cost of the paragraph is smaller than the cost of the second search, and the second search is rate-limited. AND THE REACH, WHICH THIS ROW DID NOT STATE AND SHOULD HAVE. Antenati's “cerca per nome” covers SCILLA DEATHS 1911-1943 AND NOTHING ELSE for this comune -- no births, no marriages, nothing before 1911. So every nil taken from it here, MARIANTONIA MUSOLINO above all, means only «she did not die at Scilla between 1911 and 1943 under a name the indexers transcribed that way». It is not evidence that she is absent from Scilla's registers, and the row's own conclusion -- that the only remaining route to her is Orazio Arena's second marriage act -- is the right one for that reason and not for the one given.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — the complete Scilla register list, all three fondi done/search-registry/?localita=Scilla&s_size=500 — every register Antenati holds for the comune, in one page load, to find out whether the marriage PROCESSETTI exist for the Arena weddings of 1885 and 1888.466 REGISTERS. 17 napoleonic, 182 restaurazione, 267 italiano. THE ANSWER TO THE QUESTION ASKED IS NO: there are TWENTY-TWO processetti volumes and they run 1810 to 1865 and STOP. The Italian-era fondo holds none, so the 1885 and 1888 bundles are not on Antenati and row 64's Italian half is closed. THE ANSWER TO THE QUESTION NOT ASKED IS MUCH BETTER. Francesco Antonio Arena and Serafina Arlotta married 7 FEBRUARY 1851, and the bundle for it is the volume 1849-1852, ark an_ua2002487. Processetti carry both spouses' BIRTH extracts and the DEATH acts of dead parents -- and the Scilla bundle of 1842-1845 carried a dead grandparent as well. One volume therefore attacks row 68 (Filippo Arena and Domenica Costa's deaths), row 39 (Giovanni Arlotta x Maria Costa's marriage) and row 85 (Domenico Arlotta) at once. AND SCILLA'S REGISTRATION BEGINS IN 1809, NOT 1816. This archive has been saying 1816 and planning around it -- see the caution written after Domenico Arlotta. Seven unopened years: Matrimoni and Morti 1809; Matrimoni, Morti and Nati 1810, 1811, 1812 and 1815; Matrimoni 1813-1814. All arks now in data/scilla-arks.tsv. PARAMETER TRAP, RECORDED: ?citta=Scilla returns ZERO with no error; ?localita=Scilla returns 466. Control-test before believing a nil.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Matrimoni processetti 1849–1852, fascicolo 13 of 1851, read in full doneThe marriage bundle of FRANCESCO ANTONIO ARENA and SERAFINA ARLOTTA, 7 February 1851 — found by listing Scilla's registers, buying one container, and locating the fascicolo wrapper by SHAPE in a thumbnail montage.FOUR SHEETS, AND THE FIRST TWO ARE WORTH THE WHOLE DAY. FOGLIO 1 — FRANCESCO ANTONIO ARENA'S BIRTH ACT, act 186 of 1819, the ACT and not the Tavola this archive has been citing. 25 November 1819, twelve o'clock, in the family's own house. FILIPPO ARENA, DI ANNI VENTICINQUE, MARINARO -- born about 1794 -- and DOMENICA COSTA, DI ANNI VENTISETTE -- born about 1792. Two generation-seven people who had no dates at all now have ages, from an act in which Filippo appeared in person. AND HE SIGNED IT, in a register where almost every act ends «perche illetterati». Baptised the next day at the Parrocchia di Scilla. FOGLIO 2 — SERAFINA ARLOTTA'S BIRTH ACT, AND IT IS ACT 89, NOT ACT 2. This archive cites «Nati 1830 atto 2» in three places; 2 is a Tavola row number. The date holds: born 13 JUNE 1830 AT TWO IN THE NIGHT in her parents' house, declared next afternoon by her father. MARIA COSTA, DI ANNI VENTITRE -- born about 1807, her first date of any kind. And GIOVANNI ARLOTTA, DI ANNI QUARANTA -- born about 1790, which CONFLICTS BY ELEVEN YEARS with his own 1854 declaration of seventy-five. Flagged, not averaged. Witness DOMENICO COSTA, commerciante, 46, produced by the father: a candidate for Maria Costa's own father and nothing more. WHAT THE BUNDLE DOES NOT CONTAIN IS ALSO EVIDENCE. No death act of any parent, where fascicolo 3 of the same year binds in «Atto di morte del padre e dell'avo paterno dello Sposo». The ordinary reading is that all four parents were alive on 7 February 1851. Graded inferred, because the bride was twenty and no consent sheet is listed either. NAVIGATION, WHICH IS THE REUSABLE PART. The volume is 1845 images over four years and its interior order is loose. Each fascicolo opens with a WRAPPER — nearly blank left page, short centred list on the right — and wrappers are findable BY SHAPE in a montage of thumbnails at width 430, with no text read at all. In 1851 they sit at images 1100 (num. 3), 1190 (num. 13), 1280 (num. 24), 1430 (num. 37). DO NOT navigate by the «Num. d'ordine» on the atti di notificazione bound inside: that is a separate publication series running at a different rate, and following it walks you into the wrong month.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — Giovanni Arlotta x Maria Costa's marriage: three processetti volumes sized, then rate-limited partThe marriage that would settle Giovanni Arlotta's eleven-year age conflict and decide whether the Domenico Arlotta generation belongs to this family. Serafina Arlotta was born 13 June 1830, so the wedding is 1829 or earlier; the candidate bundles are Scilla processetti 1828-1831 and 1824-1827.NOT FOUND YET, AND THE GROUND IS PREPARED. CONTAINERS BOUGHT AND SAVED: 1828-1831 = LD6PQKn (1296 images), 1824-1827 = 5xYoOBO (1416 images), and 1849-1852 = 58rXBoX (1845 images). YEAR MAP FOR LD6PQKn, read off a ten-point thumbnail scan: 1830 runs roughly images 700-1000 -- wrappers dated «Anno 1830» sit at images 740 and 880 -- which puts 1829 at roughly 450-700 and 1828 before that. THE VOLUME HAS NO YEAR INDEX: its last six images are ordinary documents, not a tavola, so the fascicolo wrappers are the only finding aid. THEN THE RATE LIMITER. Three ark lookups for the Scilla MARRIAGE registers of 1828, 1829 and 1830 -- the cheaper route, because an annual marriage Tavola gives the act number and the fascicolo number follows it -- ALL THREE RETURNED «403 Forbidden». Caught by the control test and recorded as a block, NOT as three absent registers. That distinction is the whole reason the test exists. The arks are in scilla-arks.tsv (1828 an_ua2002545, 1829 an_ua2002548, 1830 an_ua2002551); only the containers are owed, and they cost one paced page load each once the block lifts. 20 Sep 2026NAA — Antonino Crupi's aliens registration card, read in full; and the whole CRUPI name index doneNameSearch on CRUPI across ALL record categories — 233 items, pulled 200 to a page and read end to end — then the D4028 card for Antonino Crupi, both sides, at full resolution.THE CARD. NAA: D4028, CRUPI ANTONIO, item 9209610, digitised and free. Certificate Q 5949 / Q 7838. ANTONINO CRUPI, born 5 OCTOBER 1907 at PIEDIMONTE ETNEO, ITALY. A BOOT REPAIRER. Address Box 484, Ingham; Abbott Street, Ingham. MARRIED. Entered Australia 2 SEPTEMBER 1930, PORT OF DEBARKATION BRISBANE. Five feet four; eyes black; hair black; build sturdy; NOTABLE MARKS: THE FOURTH FINGER OF THE RIGHT HAND DEFORMED. Registered 3 OCTOBER 1939 at INGHAM police station. HE WAS INTERNED, AND THAT IS THE THIRD MAN. The front is stamped LOVEDAY 35924. The CHANGE OF ADDRESS panel on the reverse reads «ex Gay[thorne] to 14A 16.3.43», then «LCCE 1/207 2.4.43 MAIL EXTRACT to wife, Q'land», then «22/10/43 Released and returned to Queensland». So: moved into Loveday compound 14A in March 1943 and released seven months later. ROSARIO MAZZA was interned on his own dossier; MARIO CRUPI at Loveday from 18 August 1942 to 30 December 1943; ANTONINO CRUPI released 22 October 1943. Angela Crupi's husband and two of her brothers, all three behind wire in the same eighteen months. AND THE CENSOR COPIED A SENTENCE OF HIS ONTO HIS REGISTRATION CARD. The mail extract of 2 April 1943, from a letter to his wife in Queensland, is quoted on the card: «... Our freedom will come soon and we shall always cry Vive Italia.» It is the only sentence of his own that this archive has, and it survives because an official wrote it down as evidence against him. Read across two overlapping enlargements; the middle words are the least certain. PIETRO HAS NO CARD, AND THE REASON IS A FINDING. NAA: BP4/3 ITALIAN CRUPI P: «Alien Registration Certificate NOT ISSUED», the folio noting he was NATURALISED 23 FEBRUARY 1932. By 1939 he was a British subject and outside the series entirely. THE TWO BROTHERS CAME TOGETHER. NAA: BP9/3 ITALIAN CRUPI P and CRUPI A both read «arrived in Brisbane on OTRANTO 19 AUGUST 1930» -- Pietro born 1898, Antonino born 1907. Antonino's own card gives his entry as 2 September, two weeks after the ship docked: the same two-true-dates pattern already recorded for the passenger index. And BP9/3 ITALIAN CRUPI M names MARIO'S ship: «Crupi, Mariano born 1905 -- arrived in Brisbane on MARIA CRISTINA 20 MAY 1928», against the 13 June 1928 on his card. TWO NATURALISATIONS. A714 57/18792: ANTONINO naturalised 7 NOVEMBER 1946, born 1907 at Piedimonte, RESIDENT AT TULLY, wife NELLIE. A714 9/4300: MARIO naturalised 26 APRIL 1940, born 1905, resident at Ingham, SINGLE. THE TRAP, AND IT IS ONE BARCODE AWAY. Item 9209609 is ALSO a D4028 card for a CRUPI ANTONIO -- born at SERRATA, arrived MELBOURNE 20 October 1926. Serrata is in Calabria, not Sicily. Three consecutive barcodes, 9209609 / 9209610 / 9209611, and the middle and last are this family while the first is a stranger with the same name. Adjacency in an alphabetical card series is not kinship. ONE CONFLICT LEFT OPEN. The card says MARRIED in October 1939, and this archive notes an Antonino Crupi marrying in Queensland on 24 AUGUST 1940. Either that marriage is another man, or «married» was written on the card later. Not resolved here. UNREAD AND WANTED: NAA: BP242/1 Q16634, «Crupi, Antonio and Maria -- Queensland investigation case file», 1939-1957, Brisbane, ACCESS OPEN. That is very likely Antonino's own security file and it is the document behind the internment.

PP14/3 ITALIAN/PROSTAMO G: GABRIELE PROSTAMO, Perth, application for registration as alien, CIRCA 1916-1920 (5137304). PP14/3 ITALIAN/PROSTAMO F: FRANCESCO PROSTAMO, Perth, same series, CIRCA 1916-1920 (5137303). J25 1971/1074: Mazza, Sebastiano; wife Domenica [nee Prostamo] (5121679). J25 1962/5930: Prostamo, Domenico Francesco; wife ASSUNTE MARIA [nee MELLUSO] (1682238). J25 1972/23086: Melluso, Gregorio; wife Carmela; and children Giuseppe, Maria [surname PROSTAMO] (5072628). J25 1961/3716: PROSTAMO, Antonino Giacomo and Angela [nee TOMSIC] (12901469). J25 1963/2810: Prostamo, Francesco (1681780). B44 V1974/35793: Prostamo, Francesca, Melbourne (4011946). B44 V1970/39910: Prostamo, R, Melbourne (14758061). THE CORRECTION THIS SET FORCES. This archive has Francesco Prostamo arriving at FREMANTLE on 10 OCTOBER 1957, graded «documented» because the NAA passenger index, the ship and the embarkation port all agreed. THE BRISBANE ALIENS SERIES SAYS HE ARRIVED AT BRISBANE ON 21 OCTOBER. Both are true and they answer different questions: 10 October is when the ROMA first touched Australia, 21 October is when he got off it. The family settled in Queensland, and the port of debarkation is the one that says so. Same for Francesca, on the same voyage. A NAME TO WATCH, NOT TO MERGE. The catalogue calls her «TRUNGADI [nee Prostamo], Francesca». The manifest, read in the original, makes the Francesca on that voyage FRANCESCO'S DAUGHTER, BORN 1945 AT BRIATICO, AGED TWELVE -- and a twelve-year-old is not «nee» anything. The likeliest reading is that the registration card was annotated with a married name years later and the catalogue transcribed the card's final state. TRUNGADI is therefore a married surname to look for, not a second woman. AND TWO PROSTAMO IN WESTERN AUSTRALIA IN THE FIRST WORLD WAR. PP14/3 holds alien registration applications for GABRIELE PROSTAMO and FRANCESCO PROSTAMO at Perth, CIRCA 1916-1920 -- thirty years earlier than anything this archive holds in Australia, and in the wrong state. Unread.
20 Sep 2026NAA NameSearch — the complete POLISTENA set, fifty items, separated from the town of the same name doneNameSearch, all record categories, on POLISTENA, read out in full at 200 to a page.FIFTY ITEMS, AND THE FIRST THING TO DO WITH THEM IS SPLIT THEM IN TWO. POLISTENA IS A COMUNE IN REGGIO CALABRIA AS WELL AS A SURNAME, and NameSearch does not know the difference: four A714 naturalisations come back for men named OLIMPIO, MARFEA, FOGLIADORO and PALESTRI whose PLACE OF BIRTH is Polistena, and three A7919 prisoner-of-war records for SAVARESE, ROSSI and BOTTIGLIERI, born there and captured in Africa. THIS ARCHIVE'S POLISTENA ARE FROM SCILLA, seventy kilometres away. Seven of the fifty are the town, not the family. THREE HITS THAT MATTER. ONE. J25 1971/3900, Brisbane, 1953-1971: «ARENA, ROCCO; WIFE ANGELINA [OR ANGELA] [NEE POLISTENA]». Row 80 of the work list says their marriage «is not documented anywhere in this archive». HERE IS A COMMONWEALTH FILE THAT NAMES THEM AS HUSBAND AND WIFE AND GIVES HER MAIDEN NAME. It does not date the marriage; it proves it and it names the file that may. Not yet examined, held at Brisbane. TWO. BP9/3, ITALIAN POLISTENA G: «Polistena, GIOVANNI BORN 1883 - arrived in BRISBANE on the ORMONDE on 21 FEBRUARY 1928». This archive's GIOVANNI POLISTENA was born at Scilla in 1883, act 206, and is already documented sailing from PALERMO to NEW ORLEANS on the LIGURIA in February 1908, on the line immediately after Michele Donato. A SECOND MIGRATION, TWENTY YEARS LATER AND TO THE OTHER SIDE OF THE WORLD, would explain how this family reached Queensland at all. The year of birth matches exactly and the port is the right one. IT IS A CANDIDATE AND NOT A FINDING: the entry carries no parents and no birthplace. The test is the item itself, or a Queensland record placing him there in the 1930s. THREE. TWO MORE DONATO-POLISTENA MARRIAGES, IN QUEENSLAND THIS TIME. J25 1967/9873: «Polistena, Giuseppe; wife MARIA MADDALENA [NEE DONATO]». J25 1970/3023: «DONATO, CONCETTA [NEE POLISTENA]». THE SAME LESSON THE 1908 Scilla death index taught about Serafina Arlotta, in a different archive on the other side of the world: A MARRIED WOMAN IS INDEXED UNDER WHICHEVER NAME THE CLERK HAD, and a sweep that only reads the maiden surname column will miss her. Here the catalogue happens to carry both.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Matrimoni 1828 atto 28 and the annual Tavola, read in full doneGiovanni Arlotta x Maria Costa's marriage — the act that would settle an eleven-year conflict in his age and decide whether the Domenico Arlotta generation belongs to this family. Found from the annual Tavola after 1829 was cleared and 1830 was not needed.ACT 28 OF 1828, AND IT SETTLES MORE THAN IT WAS ASKED TO. Scilla, 30 AUGUST 1828: GIOVANNI ARLOTTA, DI ANNI VENTOTTO, marinaro, FIGLIO DI FU DOMENICO ARLOTTA E DI ANNA BARBARO; and MARIA COSTA, D'ANNI VENTISEI, FIGLIA DI ROCCO DI COSTA, marinaro, E DI PASQUALINA IDONE. Banns posted Sunday 3 August; church wedding 7 SEPTEMBER 1828 before D. Domenico Zanghi and Bruno Ingrigneri. THE BRIDE'S PARENTS ATTENDED AND CONSENTED IN PERSON -- so Rocco Costa and Pasqualina Idone were both alive and in the room. DOMENICO ARLOTTA IS CONFIRMED AND DATED. He is «FU Domenico» here, so DEAD BEFORE 30 AUGUST 1828, twenty-six years tighter than the «before April 1854» this archive carried -- and the generation is not dislodged, which was the real risk. THREE NEW PEOPLE: ANNA BARBARO, Giovanni's mother; ROCCO COSTA and PASQUALINA IDONE, Maria's parents. All three alive in 1828, none in the family tree. AND IT BREAKS TWO AGES, ONE OF THEM WRITTEN BY ME THIS MORNING. Giovanni Arlotta is TWENTY-EIGHT here (b. c. 1800), FORTY on his daughter's birth act of 1830, and SEVENTY-FIVE on his grandson's of 1854 -- three of his own declarations spanning twenty-one years. The marriage act is preferred. Twenty-eight in 1828 is thirty in 1830, and «trenta» written or read as «quaranta» is ordinary; the 1854 «settantacinque» is not, and stays open as either a badly wrong age or a second Giovanni Arlotta son of a late Domenico. MARIA COSTA is TWENTY-SIX here (b. c. 1802) and this archive entered her as «c. 1807» a few hours earlier off the 1830 act. CORRECTED, WITH THE OLD FIGURE LEFT ON THE RECORD. A CANDIDATE WITHDRAWN BY THE SAME ACT. A DOMENICO COSTA, commerciante, forty-six, witnessed Serafina's registration in 1830 and was recorded here as a candidate for Maria Costa's father, graded «candidate» precisely so it could be withdrawn. HER FATHER WAS ROCCO. That is what the grade was for. AND THE TAVOLA CLOSED ANOTHER ROW FOR FREE. The 1828 annual index names BOTH SPOUSES' PARENTS, and its FIRST entry is «ARENA ANTONIO e DE LUCA SERAFINA -- ORAZIO e MORABITO LETTERIA / FILIPPO e ALFONZETTI ANNA -- 10 Ag.o 1828 -- atto 26». That is the OTHER Antonio Arena married to a Serafina, the collision guarded this morning; his parents are ORAZIO ARENA and LETTERIA MORABITO, which is where that family's Orazio comes from. 1829's Tavola opens at B with no A section at all, so neither surname married that year.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla, Matrimoni 1851 Tavola annuale, entry 1, against the 1828 convention doneA direct test of a correction graded «inferred» that morning: were Filippo Arena, Domenica Costa, Giovanni Arlotta and Maria Costa alive when their children married in February 1851? The processetti bundle held no death act for any of them, which is an argument from absence.CONFIRMED, AND BY A POSITIVE READING. Container LD6brxB, 96 images; the 1851 Tavola runs images 88-96 and ENTRY 1 IS «ARENA FRANCESCO e ARLOTTA SERAFINA», Scilla. Its parents column reads «FILIPPO, e COSTA DOMENICA» and «GIOVANNI e COSTA MARIA» -- both exactly right, and NEITHER CARRIES «FU». WHY THAT IS EVIDENCE AND NOT SILENCE. The same series marks the dead: the 1828 Tavola writes Giovanni Arlotta as son of «FU Dom.co e Barbaro Anna» -- father dead, mother living -- and «fu» appears on THIS PAGE of 1851 in other entries, including entry 4. The convention is in use locally and it is not applied to our four. THEY WERE ALL FOUR ALIVE IN FEBRUARY 1851. The morning's correction moves from «inferred» to «documented». AND THE INSTRUMENT IS BETTER THAN THIS ARCHIVE HAS BEEN TREATING IT. A Scilla marriage Tavola carries Num. d'ordine | Sposi | Patria | PROFESSIONE | GENITORI | Giorno della Celebrazione. One index opening therefore gives four parents AND dates the life status of all four, without opening an act. Every year of the restaurazione series will do this. ONE CAUTION FOR WHOEVER USES IT. The date column is the CHURCH celebration, not the civil act -- in 1828 the Tavola says 7 September where the civil promise was 30 August. An act number and a Tavola date will disagree by days or weeks and neither is wrong. AND THE ROWS ARE NOT CLEANLY ALIGNED: in 1851 the parents are written slightly above their own line and interleave with the neighbouring entry, so a parent pair must be matched to a spouse BY NAME, not by height on the page. Entry 1's four lines were disentangled that way.
20 Sep 2026Antenati — Scilla marriage indexes 1810, 1811, 1812, 1818, 1819 swept for Filippo Arena partFILIPPO ARENA x DOMENICA COSTA'S MARRIAGE, which is the only route left to GENERATION NINE on the Scilla side. He was twenty-five and she twenty-seven when their son Francesco was born on 25 November 1819, so they married about 1812-1819. Four volumes' indexes checked; two more have none.TWO CLEAN NEGATIVES AND A COVERAGE MAP. 1819 (container 0AJEJ8b, 79 images, Tavola at 75-79): alphabetised by SURNAME. Its A section holds ONE entry -- «Abbate Grazia e Capitano Giovanna», 8 Febraro. NO ARENA. 1818 (container LPaGar8, 43 images, Tavola at 41-43): alphabetised by the GROOM'S GIVEN NAME, exactly as this archive's cautions warn. FILIPPO THEREFORE SITS UNDER F, NOT A, and the F section is entries 10-13: Francesco Migliarino x Antonia Fulco (21 Gennaro), Francesco Polimeni x Clementina Perina (26 Gennaro), FILIPPO PIZZARELLO x Francesca Fava (27 Gennaro), Francesco La Valle x Nicola Florio (10 Aprile). NO FILIPPO ARENA. Searching the A section of 1818 would have produced a confident, complete and entirely false negative. 1817 (container 5K9P9J9, 43 images) HAS NO TAVOLA AT ALL -- the volume runs to act 41 and then blank forms. 1816 (container wQYJYDb, 45 images) LIKEWISE: acts to 42, then blanks, and the archivist's card reads VOLUME TARLATO, worm-eaten. SO THE UNREAD GROUND IS: 1816 and 1817, which cost a full act-by-act read of about forty openings apiece because there is no index to shortcut; and the NAPOLEONIC years 1809-1815, whose containers have not been bought (arks in scilla-arks.tsv). Scilla's registers begin in 1809, not 1816, so those years exist. ALL FOUR CONTAINERS ARE NOW SAVED so no later session pays for them again. EXTENDED THE SAME DAY INTO THE NAPOLEONIC FONDO, AND THREE MORE YEARS FALL. 1812 (container wX9PERp, 23 images, Tavola at 22-23): SEVENTEEN marriages, alphabetised by the GROOM'S GIVEN NAME. All seventeen read. NO FILIPPO ARENA. 1811 (container wl1gvZD, 19 images, Tavola at 18-19): same ordering. Its F section is Ferdinando di Franco, Francesco Arbitrio and Fulcantonio Bova. NO FILIPPO ARENA. 1810 (container LzjyVKj, 18 images, INDICE at 17-18): same ordering. Its F section is Filippo LAGANA, Francesco Vita, Francesco Martello, Francesco Zirilli and a Francesco Fa[va]. THERE IS A FILIPPO AND HE IS A LAGANA. NO FILIPPO ARENA. 1809 (container LD6vJel, 29 images) HAS NO TAVOLA -- acts to 27, then blank forms. SO THE MARRIAGE IS NOT IN 1810, 1811, 1812, 1818 OR 1819, and the unindexed years left are 1809, 1813-1814, 1815, 1816 and 1817. ALL NINE CONTAINERS ARE NOW SAVED. AND THE SWEEP PAID FOR ITSELF SIDEWAYS. The 1811 Tavola gives «GIOVANNI VILARDI x CANDIDA COSTA -- Gaetano ed Antonia Mellica / ROCCO e PASQUALINA IDONE». Rocco Costa and Pasqualina Idone are MARIA COSTA'S PARENTS, read three hours earlier off the 1828 marriage act. SO CANDIDA COSTA IS MARIA COSTA'S SISTER, married at Scilla in 1811 -- a generation-seven sibling found for free, in an index opened for somebody else. FOUR MORE SCILLA HOUSEHOLDS NOTED IN PASSING, none of them joined to this family: CARLO ARENA x EUGENIA GRAJA, whose daughter Giovanna married Giovanni La Mussa in January 1812; a «fu ANTONIA ARENA» named as a mother in 1812; NATALINA ARENA, a mother in 1811; and ANNA ARLOTTA, a mother in 1811. Carlo Arena is of roughly the right generation to be Filippo Arena's father and there is NOTHING WHATEVER to support that beyond the surname and the town -- recorded so the next session does not rediscover him and think it has found something.

He was naturalised in Queensland TWENTY-FIVE YEARS EARLIER, and naturalisation of that period wanted about five years' residence, so he was in Australia by about 1929. He is not a post-war arrival and not a stranger passing through. IT STILL DOES NOT MAKE HIM A RELATIVE -- the certificate names no parents -- but it turns a single roll line into a man with two dates.
